Extends `permit_document` with `authority_role` and `governs_enforceable_obligations` fields.\n\n3. Removes the blanket `permit_document_text_not_available` unresolved and replaces it with per-document unresolved entries only for kinds whose authority role the packet does not establish (`minor_modification`, `reasonable_potential`, unknown kinds).\n\nProduction `construction.py` is unchanged.\n","message":"Wrote contents to /tmp/world-experiment/npdes-obligation-targeted-resolution-v1/0d8ffdfcc9f01d3a6c84fc6b405d8f4e/REVIEW.md"}}},"hookAdditionalContexts":[],"toolCallId":"tool_63903fae-2441-4d92-a329-6104861a7bd","startedAtMs":"1788618050747","completedAtMs":"1788618056192"},"model_call_id":"5692b3d4-c96f-4f25-9723-b045432213f0-6-1ikw","session_id":"5914fde2-c258-4ee0-9c6f-e2d43e672a3e","timestamp_ms":1788618056353}

_declare_purpose_requirements(\n    purpose: Purpose,\n    limit_rows: list[dict[str, str]],\n    documents: list[dict[str, str]],\n) -> None:\n    purpose.require_materializable(\n        \"fy2025_measurements_materializable\",\n        relation=\"fy2025_measurement\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_materializable(\n        \"measurement_limit_pairs_materializable\",\n        relation=\"measurement_limit_pair\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_unique(\n        \"unique_applicable_limit_per_measurement\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n        candidates=\"measurement_limit_pair\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        cardinality=\"ONE\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_materializable(\n        \"numeric_comparison_candidates_materializable\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_numeric(\n        \"limit_value_for_comparison\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"limit_value_standard_units\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_numeric(\n        \"reported_value_for_comparison\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"dmr_value_standard_units\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"limit_comparison_operator\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"limit_value_qualifier_code\",\n        known=[\"<=\", \">=\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"reported_value_qualifier\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"dmr_value_qualifier_code\",\n        known=[\"=\", \"<\", \">\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"optional_monitoring_flag_for_limit_applicability\",\n        relation=\"measurement_limit_pair\",\n        field=\"optional_monitoring_flag\",\n        known=[\"Y\", \"N\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"limit_type_code_for_enforceability\",\n        relation=\"measurement_limit_pair\",\n        field=\"limit_type_code\",\n        known=[\"ENF\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"statistical_base_for_limit_comparison\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"statistical_base_type_code\",\n        known=[\"AVG\", \"MAX\", \"MIN\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n\n    purpose.require_materializable(\n        \"monitoring_requirements_materializable\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n        purpose=\"B\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"monitoring_frequency_code\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n        field=\"limit_freq_of_analysis_code\",\n        purpose=\"B\",\n        per=\"permit_limit_row\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"limit_sample_type_code\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n        field=\"limit_sample_type_code\",\n        purpose=\"B\",\n        per=\"permit_limit_row\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"optional_monitoring_flag_for_obligation\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n        field=\"optional_monitoring_flag\",\n        known=[\"Y\", \"N\"],\n        purpose=\"B\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"permit_limit_comment_text\",\n        relation=\"permit_limit\",\n        field=\"dmr_comment_text\",\n        known=[\"\"],\n        purpose=[\"B\", _declare_purpose_requirements(\n    purpose: Purpose,\n    limit_rows: list[dict[str, str]],\n    documents: list[dict[str, str]],\n) -> None:\n    purpose.require_materializable(\n        \"fy2025_measurements_materializable\",\n        relation=\"fy2025_measurement\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_materializable(\n        \"measurement_limit_pairs_materializable\",\n        relation=\"measurement_limit_pair\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_unique(\n        \"unique_applicable_limit_per_measurement\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n        candidates=\"measurement_limit_pair\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        cardinality=\"ONE\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_materializable(\n        \"numeric_comparison_candidates_materializable\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_numeric(\n        \"limit_value_for_comparison\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"limit_value_standard_units\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_numeric(\n        \"reported_value_for_comparison\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"dmr_value_standard_units\",\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"limit_comparison_operator\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"limit_value_qualifier_code\",\n        known=[\"<=\", \">=\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"reported_value_qualifier\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"dmr_value_qualifier_code\",\n        known=[\"=\", \"<\", \">\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"optional_monitoring_flag_for_limit_applicability\",\n        relation=\"measurement_limit_pair\",\n        field=\"optional_monitoring_flag\",\n        known=[\"Y\", \"N\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"limit_type_code_for_enforceability\",\n        relation=\"measurement_limit_pair\",\n        field=\"limit_type_code\",\n        known=[\"ENF\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"statistical_base_for_limit_comparison\",\n        relation=\"numeric_comparison_candidate\",\n        field=\"statistical_base_type_code\",\n        known=[\"AVG\", \"MAX\", \"MIN\"],\n        purpose=\"A\",\n        per=\"measurement\",\n    )\n\n    purpose.require_materializable(\n        \"monitoring_requirements_materializable\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n        purpose=\"B\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"monitoring_frequency_code\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n        field=\"limit_freq_of_analysis_code\",\n        purpose=\"B\",\n        per=\"permit_limit_row\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"limit_sample_type_code\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n        field=\"limit_sample_type_code\",\n        purpose=\"B\",\n        per=\"permit_limit_row\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"optional_monitoring_flag_for_obligation\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n        field=\"optional_monitoring_flag\",\n        known=[\"Y\", \"N\"],\n        purpose=\"B\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"permit_limit_comment_text\",\n        relation=\"permit_limit\",\n        field=\"dmr_comment_text\",\n        known=[\"\"],\n        purpose=[\"B\", \"C\"],\n        per=\"permit_limit_row\",\n    )\n    purpose.require_interpreted(\n        \"limit_set_designator_for_schedule\",\n        relation=\"monitoring_requirement_fy2025\",\n
